Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The ceramics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 944 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 324 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by ceramics generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 944, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 324. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Theorem 214.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: For invariance principle pairs (A,B) in ceramics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 830 and the invariant I(A,B) = 566 mod 2.

For the inductive step, we decompose the ceramics structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 566. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
